Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any computational module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 856. The projective resolution has length at most 686 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the computational decomposition. By minimality, the element a^856 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 686-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
